  ISO-text timestamps across intake, outbox, and queue, including replay and owner isolation.
  The complete repository gate passed, and source `ff3689c` is now deployed on unweighted Gateway
  alias `49` at state `271`. The exact two-update/no-replacement release passed immutable artifact,
  direct API-boundary, and refresh-backed zero-drift verification. The public policy still returns
  HTTP `200` with `no-store` and the unchanged Greenhouse-only expiry. No extension rebuild,
  sign-out, or account-held-item removal is required. The 2026-09-05 installed follow-up now shows
  zero pending, zero stopped, and two account-held items. That alone does not prove acceptance:
  acknowledged delivery and local retention expiry both remove pending items. The inspected
  post-release logs contain no contribution-store failures, while 19 scheduled batches spanning
  the release/follow-up window report zero claimed/completed/parked/retried/dead contributions.
  Those earlier aggregates did not establish acknowledgement or publication. The subsequent
  owner-refreshed receipt now confirms one saved contribution in `awaiting_independent_verification`.
  A later successful scheduled batch independently reports one claimed and one parked contribution,
  with zero completed, retried, dead, or delivery-published items. Installed intake and the owner
  processing-status read are now confirmed; the server delivery failure is resolved. Independent
  provider verification remains unresolved. The latest fixed aggregate identifies only description
  mismatch on two held submissions. Refresh reads status only; it does not requeue parked work.
  Do not resubmit the acknowledged contribution. Trust, publication, and remaining lifecycle
  acceptance are not implied by this successful intake.
  The 2026-09-05 completion pass has locally verified regression-backed provider compatibility
  for omitted optional location and exact location/work-type separation, plus bounded
  aggregate-only provider-hold inspection and snapshot-fenced recovery. Repository checks and
  fresh-PostgreSQL regressions pass. Source `46a8076` is deployed at state `272` on scheduled alias
  `8` and Trust alias `7`, with exact artifact checks, zero drift, and 13/13 alarms OK. Two mismatch
  holds were safely requeued but returned to mismatch; fixed field-level diagnostics are the next
  deployment, not a claim of resolution. Diagnostic source `e0ff15d` is now deployed at state `273`
  on scheduled/Trust aliases `9`/`8`; both holds have only company/description mismatches. Corrective
  provider-company and encoded-HTML mapping passed 127 ingestion tests and full repository checks,
  and is deployed at state `274` on scheduled/Trust aliases `10`/`9`, with zero drift. Both submissions
  remained mismatch-held. Narrow legacy-preview compatibility source `ed8dd9a` now passes 133
  ingestion tests and full repository checks and is deployed at state `275`, scheduled/Trust `11`/`10`,
  with exact artifacts and zero drift. The latest bounded retry claimed/parked both records, with
  zero completion/retry/dead/publication; company mismatch is resolved, and only description
  mismatch remains in two aggregate events. A bounded read-only current public-page/API comparison
  also differs beyond whitespace, so no broader matching relaxation is justified. No submitted
  content was rewritten, and no raw page or posting values were retained. The signed development
  policy subsequently expired and was then renewed on explicit user approval through
  `2026-09-06T19:24:54.670Z` with unchanged Greenhouse-only scope. Renewal did not manually retry
  either held contribution; description verification remains open. D2-024 records the separate
  warm-extension policy-readiness refresh gap found during renewal; this policy-only change does
  not claim to fix that client behavior. The compatibility preserves provider truth and rejects
  arbitrary excerpts/aliases.
  Human review boundaries stay unchanged. The phase document
  now contains one explicit outstanding acceptance/input table.
  Remaining gates are the D1
  prerequisites; authorized provider fixtures and QA disposition; completion of Chrome parity,

  approval; authenticated branded-browser contribution/lifecycle and human assistive-technology
  review; authenticated contribution/processing load and abuse, alert-recipient acceptance, and

  keyboard, delivery-state, and two-surface axe coverage passes. The `/extension-support` source

| 2026-07-28 | Use Auth0 as the initial managed OIDC provider while preserving `issuer + subject` identity mapping and a controlled dual-provider migration path. | Auth0 is the strongest direct fit for the accepted multi-client OIDC/PKCE contract; provider selection must not become product-data lock-in. | D0 onward | [ADR-012](../../adr/012-auth0-initial-managed-identity.md), [Auth0 baseline](../../security/auth0-configuration-baseline.md) |
| 2026-07-28 | Use `us-east-2` and keep all phases lean until launch review, security/recovery requirements, or measured production demand justifies scaling. | Preserve quality and the architectural scale path while avoiding idle replicas, ALB, Redis, Multi-AZ data, NAT, and continuously provisioned staging. | D0–D7 | [ADR-013](../../adr/013-lean-until-demand-cloud-runtime.md), [delivery rules](README.md#tracking-rules) |
| 2026-07-29 | Defer real mobile OIDC application/device conformance to D6; it no longer blocks D0. | Current implementation is web-focused, and mobile release must be tested against the actual D6 client rather than only its foundation shell. | D0, D6 | [ADR-012 amendment](../../adr/012-auth0-initial-managed-identity.md), [Auth0 conformance matrix](../../security/auth0-development-conformance.md) |
| 2026-07-29 | Remove mandatory representative-user research and the fixed 20-journey quota from D0. | Pre-product participant recruitment is not needed to prove the foundation; synthetic/deployed quality evidence remains mandatory and optional later research retains consent safeguards. | D0 | [Phase 0 tasks](phase-0-foundation.md), [optional research protocol](../foundation/research-plan.md) |
| 2026-07-30 | Temporarily permit synthetic-only staging to trust the exact development Auth0 issuer while the Free subscription provides one tenant. | Exercise isolated AWS and Temporal staging without misrepresenting shared provider users, keys, logs, administration, and failure modes as identity isolation. Dedicated staging/production tenants and replay remain launch gates. | D0, D6 | [ADR-014](../../adr/014-shared-development-auth0-for-synthetic-staging.md), [Phase 6 tasks](phase-6-mobile-and-paid.md) |
| 2026-07-31 | Require an independent open-book security assessment and penetration test of the exact launch candidate before public release, then repeat it annually and after material triggers. | Intentional public endpoints must be bounded, while every candidate, object, function, property, service, admin, webhook, and business-flow boundary must reject unauthorized use; internal tests and scanners do not independently prove the deployed system. | D0, D1–D7, public launch and operation | [ADR-015](../../adr/015-independent-prelaunch-security-review.md), [API security and final-review baseline](../../security/api-security-and-final-review.md), [D6 task](phase-6-mobile-and-paid.md), [D7 task](phase-7-quality-and-expansion.md) |
| 2026-07-31 | Replace the continuously running Fargate gateway and general workflow worker with a scale-to-zero Lambda application runtime before completing D0. | Preserve the proven contracts, durability, isolation, recovery, and maintenance model without paying for idle application processes; retain Fargate only where an on-demand container/browser boundary is technically warranted. | D0 onward | [ADR-016](../../adr/016-scale-to-zero-application-runtime.md), [D0-016 plan](phase-0-serverless-runtime-migration.md) |
| 2026-08-03 | Stop confirmed zero-traffic development and synthetic-staging ECS application tasks before Lambda cutover while retaining their rollback resources. | Environment availability currently protects no user; a zero-work, checksum-reviewed, Terraform-owned idle posture releases continuous compute/public IPs without prematurely deleting the rollback boundary. | D0 | [ADR-017](../../adr/017-zero-traffic-precutover-ecs-idle-posture.md), [D0-016 plan](phase-0-serverless-runtime-migration.md) |
| 2026-08-10 | Keep development continuously usable and operate synthetic staging through guarded active, quiesced, and dormant states. | Preserve isolated release evidence while removing the staging RDS and three hourly interface endpoints between bounded tests; a simple RDS stop would auto-restart after seven days. | D0 onward | [ADR-018](../../adr/018-disposable-prelaunch-staging-data-plane.md), [D0-017 lifecycle](phase-0-staging-on-demand-runtime.md) |

## Program blockers and risks

| ID | State | Risk or blocker | Affected deliveries | Owner | Mitigation or next action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PR-001 | Open | ApplyDash is the provisional development identity; final public name/domain remain a D6 release blocker. | D6 public release | Product owner | Complete D6-012 before production OAuth/email/store/public identity activation. |
| PR-002 | Closed | D0-014 and D0-015 are removed after their review packages and the security/privacy engineering pre-audit were completed. ADR-019/ADR-020 transfer accountable counsel and independent security/privacy disposition to mandatory D6 gates without claiming approval; capability gates remain active. | D6 public launch; D1–D7 capability activation | Product owner | Complete D6-015/D6-016 before launch and every earlier capability-specific gate before activation. |
| PR-003 | Closed | D0-006/D0-008/D0-011 and D0-016 pass. Both accounts have exact `$5` budgets, healthy exports, protected evidence, attributable billing, and stable caller-account-scoped `2026-08-09` daily reads with no ECS/Fargate service charge. Current-month invoice finalization remains monthly finance reconciliation rather than an engineering gate. Dedicated staging identity and mobile conformance remain D6 gates. | D0 onward | Product owner; platform engineering | Preserve account-scoped cost inspection and complete dedicated identity/mobile gates in D6. |
| PR-004 | Open | The independent final security assessor is unassigned. Public launch cannot proceed without complete scope, exact-candidate assessment, remediation, independent retest, and signed disposition under ADR-015. | D6 public launch and material post-launch changes | Product owner; security owner | Select the independent assessor before launch-candidate freeze, agree rules of engagement and complete surface inventory, and reserve remediation/retest capacity. |
| PR-005 | Closed | Both development and staging accepted workflow releases passed checksum-authorized rollback/restoration, full conformance, alert delivery, cleanup, and zero drift. Each accepted current release is restored with triggers disabled, ECS idle, and routing unchanged. Staging authenticated gateway acceptance and the exact three-record legacy drain also pass. | D0 onward | Platform engineering | Execute and accept the controlled staging cutover; retain ECS/Temporal rollback resources until replacement acceptance and expiry. |
| PR-006 | Closed | AWS made the staging regional Lambda concurrency quota effective at `1000`, above the guarded minimum. The exact staging gateway and workflow dark deployments, zero-drift verification, and authenticated gateway acceptance passed with bounded reserved concurrency. | D0-016 / SR-006 | Platform engineering; AWS Support | Execute the controlled staging cutover; request no further capacity without measured demand. |
| PR-007 | Closed | Staging gateway reserved concurrency `5` is deployed. The fixed workload passed 500/500 public requests and 25/25 durable workflows with zero Lambda throttles and clean aggregate capacity evidence. | D0-016 / SR-006 | Platform engineering | Preserve the measured ceiling; request no additional capacity without evidence. |
| PR-008 | Closed | The merged serverless workload inspector replayed the exact accepted staging window and passed runtime, capacity, queue/DLQ, alarm, privacy-log, idle-ECS, dispatch, trigger, and zero-drift boundaries without AWS mutation. | D0-016 / SR-006 | Platform engineering | Reuse the corrected gate for development cutover and final two-environment acceptance. |
